Printed by scripts/fleet_status.py render, never composed by hand, then at most one AskUserQuestion round for the Needs-you items. Use for "fleet status", "status across repos", "what needs me", "what is stuck", "what is running everywhere". NOT for one repo's task table (/board); NOT for messaging factories. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc/skills/fleet-status))
- **fleet-status**: One-shot overview of all active and recent UTS runs across projects and accounts — a status plus resource-usage dashboard grouped by project, with a needs-attention list. Use when the user asks what is running, show all my jobs, fleet/dashboard status, usage across my projects, or wants a standup-style summary of their experiments. ([mtics/uts-compute](https://github.com/mtics/uts-compute/tree/HEAD/skills/fleet-status))
- **fleet-status**: Check the health of all machines, services, and models across the fleet. ([phyter1/seed](https://github.com/phyter1/seed/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fleet-status))
- **fleet-status**: Use for read-only fleet status queries including online/offline connectivity, idle activity, and hardware or loaded-filament inventory. ([PrintHive/print-hive-plugins](https://github.com/PrintHive/print-hive-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/print-hive/skills/fleet-status))
